- **mma-audit**: Use when the user asks to audit a spec / plan / design doc / skill file. The `subtype` field picks the criteria set. `default` (prose-coherence) is the general doc auditor. `plan` verifies a code-execution plan against the actual codebase — run this before any `mma-execute-plan` dispatch. `spec` audits requirement prose for testability and decision-trace. `skill` audits a SKILL.md against reader-effectiveness criteria. - **mmla-eval**: Evaluates the ability of LLMs and MLLMs to perform multimodal language analysis across six high-level semantic dimensions: intent, emotion, dialogue act, sentiment, speaking style, and communication behavior. It probes cross-modal reasoning and cognitive-level semantic understanding in conversational contexts using aligned text and video utterances. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MIntRec, MIntRec2.0, MELD, IEMOCAP, MOSI, CH-SIMS v2.0, UR-FUNNY-v2, MUStARD, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy (ACC). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmla-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlongbench-eval**: Evaluates long-context vision-language models across five diverse tasks: visual retrieval-augmented generation, needle-in-a-haystack retrieval/reasoning, many-shot in-context learning, document summarization, and long-document VQA. It specifically probes how model performance scales with standardized context lengths from 8K to 128K tokens when processing interleaved text and images. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLongBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports substring exact match (SubEM), accuracy (Acc). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlongbench-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlu-bbh-gsm8k-eval**: Evaluates how fine-tuning large language models on syntactically or semantically perturbed instructions impacts downstream generalization across factual knowledge, complex reasoning, and mathematical problem-solving. It also measures potential side effects on model toxicity and truthfulness under varying noise levels during both training and evaluation. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLU, BBH, GSM8K, ToxiGen, TruthfulQA, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports average test accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlu-bbh-gsm8k-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlu-bbh-ifeval-eval**: Evaluates instruction-tuned language models on factual knowledge, complex reasoning, and instruction-following capabilities. The protocol measures how different data selection methods and model sizes impact performance under strict compute budgets. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLU, BBH, IFEval, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports 5-shot accuracy, 3-shot exact match score, 0-shot accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlu-bbh-ifeval-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlu-eval**: Evaluates broad language understanding and reasoning capabilities across multiple academic and professional domains using multiple-choice questions. It tests the model's ability to process and answer questions in a few-shot setting. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLU, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports macro_avg/acc_char. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlu-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlu-sandbagging-eval**: Evaluates whether language models can strategically underperform on capability assessments by emulating a lower educational level (high school) on subject-specific questions, and measures how prompting strategies (zero-shot vs. chain-of-thought) affect this emulation. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLU, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlu-sandbagging-eval) · ★3)
- **mmlu-sr-eval**: This benchmark stress-tests the reasoning capability of large language models by replacing key terms in multiple-choice questions and answers with arbitrary dummy words and their definitions. It probes whether models rely on genuine conceptual understanding or merely on lexical memorization of pre-trained vocabulary. Performance is measured across three substitution variants to isolate the impact of context modification on reasoning robustness. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MMLU-SR, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mmlu-sr-eval) · ★3)

- **mmseqs2**: Cluster protein sequences by sequence identity using MMseqs2 and emit cluster-aware train/val/test splits. Returns assignments, a leakage report and a cluster-size plot. Reference: github.com/soedinglab/MMseqs2. Builds on the `mneme` skill (how to drive it, selected owner scopes). ([nia-e/mneme](https://github.com/nia-e/mneme/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mneme-remember) · ★2)
- **mneme-review-closure**: Triage or close an external, adversarial, or re-review finding set against Mneme with one-to-one dispositions, implementation evidence, sibling-surface attacks, and a counter-report. Use for read-only finding triage or, with repository-write authority, for saving feedback and fixing findings. Saving artifacts or editing code requires repository-write authority; creating commits requires separate explicit commit authority. Never infer either from a read-only review request. ([nia-e/mneme](https://github.com/nia-e/mneme/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mneme-review-closure) · ★2)
- **mneme-search**: Use when the user asks a factual question whose answer might live in the vault. Invokes mneme_search. Production `mneme_search` is FTS5 BM25. The experimental feature-hashed lexical-vector backend is not wired into MCP. KG enrichment is gated to summarize or timeline when full-profile graph state is active. ([OnourImpram/mneme](https://github.com/OnourImpram/mneme/tree/HEAD/packages/mneme-codex-plugin/skills/mneme-search) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **mneme-search**: Use when the user asks a factual question whose answer might live in the vault. Invokes mneme_search (FTS5 BM25, optionally fused with dense and KG backends in standard and full profiles). ([OnourImpram/mneme](https://github.com/OnourImpram/mneme/tree/HEAD/packages/mneme-cc-plugin/skills/mneme-search) · ★3)
- **mneme-seed-project**: Seed an existing project's Mneme owner with useful source-grounded orientation using ordinary SAVE. Use when the user asks to populate an empty project graph or add initial repository knowledge. Not for ordinary read-only questions, project/service setup, managed bootstrap, or automatic graph refresh. ([nia-e/mneme](https://github.com/nia-e/mneme/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/mneme-seed-project) · ★2)
- **mneme-wiki**: The Mneme knowledge tier (Tier 2) — a per-corpus wiki built from documents, read on demand and never injected into a chat. Use when ingesting a source into a wiki, querying a wiki, or auditing one. Distinct from the lean cache (Tier 1). ([Aexagon/mneme](https://github.com/Aexagon/mneme/tree/HEAD/plugins/mneme/skills/mneme-wiki) · ★1)
- **mnemex-skills**: Use mnemex before implementing a significant code change. Mnemex is a local-first decision-integrity layer: it anchors a decision to code and reports whether that decision is still fresh. ([notsointresting/mnemex](https://github.com/notsointresting/mnemex/tree/HEAD/npm/mnemex-skills) · ★1)
- **mnemic**: Keep and recall what the user tells you across conversations through the local Mnemic memory server. Use before answering anything about people, projects, places, decisions or dates, and when the user states a fact, decision, preference or correction worth keeping. ([thegreystone/mnemic](https://github.com/thegreystone/mnemic/tree/HEAD/plugin-codex/skills/mnemic) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **mnemion-doctrine**: The architectural doctrine for developing and extending the Mnemion repo — convert block-lists to chokepoints, one declarative home per invariant, fail closed, prove totality with an oracle, make the boundary un-routable in types, keep intent/structure/evolution converged. Use whenever adding or changing an invariant, security boundary, kernel pattern, feature, tool, route, or serve/egress path in this repo, or when reviewing/decomposing its code. This is the standard the codebase converged on; follow it so edits add order instead of sludge. ([daniloc/mnemion](https://github.com/daniloc/mnemion/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mnemion-doctrine) · ★22)
- **mnemo**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/mnemo) · ★745)
- **mnemo**: 과거 대화 검색, 장기기억 설정, 세션 핸드오프할 때 사용. 대화 자동 저장, 키워드 태깅, MEMORY.md 관리, 세션 전환. 관리 대상은 프로젝트 루트의 3계층(MEMORY.md + memory/ + conversations/)이며, 프로젝트 이동 후에도 기록을 함께 사용할 수 있도록 저장 경계를 검증한다. /mnemo, 므네모, 장기기억, memory, 기억해, 이전에, handoff, 핸드오프, 핸즈오프, 세션 저장 요청에 사용한다. ([Dannykkh/skill-olympus](https://github.com/Dannykkh/skill-olympus/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo) · ★9)
- **mnemo**: You have access to the project's AI coding history through mnemo. Before exploring the codebase fresh, check if relevant context exists from past sessions. ([Evidlo/dotfiles](https://github.com/Evidlo/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mnemo))
- **mnemo-a11y-checklist**: Accessibility checklist for any visual or UI change in Mnemo (tokens, shared/ui, cards, screens, hover or motion). Use it while planning a UI change and again before calling it done, even when the request only says "make it look better", "restyle", "new card" or "tweak the colors", and pair it with /check. ([Ranieeery/mnemo](https://github.com/Ranieeery/mnemo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mnemo-a11y-checklist) · ★2)
- **mnemo-cortex**: Installs and wires Mnemo Cortex (local-first persistent memory) into OpenClaw and other MCP-capable agents. Use for cross-session recall, decision history, or multi-agent shared memory. ([Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge/tree/HEAD/community/clawhub/m/mnemo-cortex) · ★3)
- **mnemo-doctor**: Diagnose and repair the mnemo installation. ALWAYS use when a mnemo MCP tool (mem_*) is missing, duplicated or failing, when a mnemo hook reports an error, when `mnemo doctor` reports warnings or errors, or after installing or upgrading mnemo. Repairs go through `mnemo doctor --fix`; never edit agent configuration by hand. ([jmeiracorbal/mnemo](https://github.com/jmeiracorbal/mnemo/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo-doctor) · ★3)
- **mnemo-loop**: Use when a session is about to dispatch, deliver, or report on mnemo's background children, or when a message arrives from another session - which verbs are the session's own and which are the maintainer's ([xyrlan/mnemo](https://github.com/xyrlan/mnemo/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo-loop) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **mnemo-memory**: Cloud-persistent memory for AI agents. Stateless plugins + TiDB Serverless = cross-session recall, multi-agent sharing, and hybrid vector + keyword search. Works with OpenClaw, Claude Code, and OpenCode. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/mnemo-memory) · ★21)
- **mnemo-memory**: ALWAYS ACTIVE when .mnemo exists — mnemo is the ONLY persistent memory for initialized projects. Never use MEMORY.md or native agent memory. Use for session start, compaction recovery, recalls, saves, and session close. Verify a valid .mnemo marker first. ([jmeiracorbal/mnemo](https://github.com/jmeiracorbal/mnemo/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo-memory) · ★3)
- **mnemo-memory**: ALWAYS ACTIVE — Persistent memory protocol. You MUST save decisions, conventions, bugs, and discoveries to mnemo proactively. Do NOT wait for the user to ask. ([MiguelAguiarDEV/mnemo](https://github.com/MiguelAguiarDEV/mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/claude-code/skills/memory))
- **mnemo-memory**: Cloud-persistent memory for AI agents. Stateless plugins + mnemo-server = cross-session recall, multi-agent sharing, and hybrid vector + keyword search. Works with OpenClaw, Claude Code, and OpenCode. ([you06/mnemos](https://github.com/you06/mnemos/tree/HEAD/clawhub-skill/mnemo-memory))
- **mnemo-memory-curation**: Detect, report, and safely repair mnemo memory conflicts. Use when Codex notices contradictory decisions, duplicate memories, stale patterns, overlapping topic keys, or when proactive memory hygiene is needed. Default to read-only review, notify the user about likely conflicts, and apply repairs only with explicit approval through official mnemo commands. ([jmeiracorbal/mnemo](https://github.com/jmeiracorbal/mnemo/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo-memory-curation) · ★3)
- **mnemo-project-maintenance**: Diagnose and safely repair duplicate mnemo project identities. Use when Codex needs to inspect `mnemo projects list`, find duplicate or stale project IDs, plan or apply `mnemo projects merge`, consolidate same-project records across UUID, path-derived, legacy, renamed, or moved directories, or perform project inventory maintenance. Default to read-only dry-run analysis and require explicit user approval before any `--yes` merge. ([jmeiracorbal/mnemo](https://github.com/jmeiracorbal/mnemo/tree/HEAD/skills/mnemo-project-maintenance) · ★3)
- **mnemo-recall**: Search past sessions for the *why* behind this project's code — design rationale, rejected alternatives, decisions, and user corrections that source never records. Use when asked why something is designed the way it is, before reconstructing a rationale from code (which produces confident-but-wrong stories), or for prior work ("did we already do this?", "how did we fix X last time?"). For what the code does now, read the source first. ([KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo](https://github.com/KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemo-recall) · ★1)
- **mnemo-replay**: Read a past turn's full content — the user prompt, the complete assistant narration, and every tool call's raw input/output — directly from the mnemo SQLite database, which stores them uncapped (recall and timeline only show a truncated slice). Bundles turn-detail.sh for one-command structured turn access with budget control. Use after recall/timeline has pointed you at a session or turn. The raw JSONL transcript is a fallback only for exact bytes or message metadata the database does not mirror. ([KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo](https://github.com/KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemo-replay) · ★1)
- **mnemo-responsive**: Responsive layout rules for Mnemo screens and panels (window sizes, sidebar rail and drawer, player layout, page margins, grids). Use when adding or changing a screen, a header, a side panel or anything whose layout depends on width, when the request mentions a small/half-screen/narrow window, snap, 4K or "it breaks when I resize". Pair it with mnemo-a11y-checklist and /check. ([Ranieeery/mnemo](https://github.com/Ranieeery/mnemo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mnemo-responsive) · ★2)
- **mnemo-timeline**: Render the temporal/decision shape of a past session - turn rows, milestones, gaps, tool bursts, compact boundary, broken-prompt candidates. Use when the user asks how a session unfolded, what the decision arc was, where a decision happened, or when reconstructing a session after compacting. ([KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo](https://github.com/KawaiiLLM/claude-mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemo-timeline) · ★1)
- **mnemoloci**: Personal second brain in an Obsidian-compatible Markdown vault (default ~/Mnemoloci). Use when the user wants to build knowledge from their projects, search what they already know, review pending pages, or check vault health — and when the user says "new project X" / "create a project" (new project folder under the projects root, e.g. from the phone), "save this link/PDF/video", "where did we leave off", "what do I have to do", "summary of the week", "turn on the backup" (or their Italian equivalents "nuovo progetto X", "crea un progetto", "salva questo link/PDF/video", "dove eravamo rimasti"… ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-backup**: Back up the Mnemoloci vault with git, optionally to a remote copy, and bring back earlier versions of notes. Use when the user says "turn on the backup", "back up my second brain", "sync the vault", "what did this page look like before", "restore yesterday's version", or, in Italian, "attiva il backup del second brain", "fai un backup", "sincronizza il vault", "com'era questa pagina prima", "ripristina la versione di ieri". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-backup) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-brief**: Write a briefing, a plain-language explanation or a progressive lesson on a topic from the user's second brain, with every claim cited and audited. Use when the user says "give me a briefing on X", "explain X in plain words", "teach me X step by step", "summarise what I know about X for a meeting", or, in Italian, "fammi un briefing su X", "spiegami X in parole semplici", "insegnami X passo passo", "riassumi quello che so su X per una riunione". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-brief) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-capture**: Save a web page, article, PDF, YouTube video, local file or pasted text into the Mnemoloci second brain, then compile it into wiki pages. Use when the user shares a link or document and says "save this", "remember this article", "add it to the second brain", "read this PDF/video and keep it", or pastes text to keep (in Italian "salva questo", "ricordati questo articolo", "aggiungi al second brain", "leggi questo PDF/video e tienilo"). ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-capture) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-changed-my-mind**: Narrate how the user's recorded position on a topic changed over time in Mnemoloci — confidence shifts, contradictions found later, revised claims. Use when the user asks things like "how did I change my mind about…", "what did I think before…", "do I still hold the same opinion about…", "why did I change my mind about…", or, in Italian, "come ho cambiato idea su…", "cosa pensavo prima di…", "sono ancora della stessa opinione su…", "perché ho cambiato idea su…". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-changed-my-mind) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-connectors**: Set up, check or fix a periodic sync connector to an external service (Google Drive, OneDrive, Gmail, GitHub, Notion) so its content flows into the vault's review queue automatically. Use when the user says "connect Google Drive/OneDrive/Gmail/GitHub/Notion", "I want to sync a folder/repo/database", "why is the connector not syncing", "how do I register the OAuth app for X", or, in Italian, "collega Google Drive/OneDrive/Gmail/GitHub/Notion", "voglio sincronizzare una cartella/repo/database", "perché il connettore non sincronizza", "come registro l'app OAuth per X". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-connectors) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-explore**: Explore what the second brain knows as a whole - people and organizations, how topics connect (hub pages, bridges, communities, surprising links, isolated pages), the wiki's quality score and trend, Obsidian maps and dashboards, llms.txt export. Use when the user asks "who are the people I work with", "how are my topics connected", "how healthy is the wiki", "make me a map of X in Obsidian", "export for another agent", or, in Italian, "chi sono le persone con cui lavoro", "come sono collegati i miei argomenti", "quanto è in salute il wiki", "fammi una mappa di X in Obsidian", "esporta per un… ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-explore) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-inbox**: Save screenshots and images into the Mnemoloci second brain — images the user attaches in the chat (also from the phone via Claude Code Remote Control), files saved by a Telegram channel, or any image path — then transcribe and compile them. Use when the user sends a screenshot/photo and says "save it", "remember it", "put it in the second brain" (in Italian "salvalo", "ricordalo", "mettilo nel second brain"), or asks to process the inbox. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-inbox) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-ingest**: Compile a user's project folders (Markdown docs, READMEs, config, code) into Mnemoloci vault pages using the two-phase plan → extract → merge → write → submit loop. Use when the user asks to read, learn, or ingest a project or folder into their second brain, to compile a registered source by name (e.g. "Compila la fonte mio-vault di Mnemoloci" or "Compile the Mnemoloci source mio-vault", the request copied from the app for a project or an Obsidian vault), or after `mnemoloci scan` shows changed files. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-ingest) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-instincts**: Learn small durable rules ("when X, do Y") from the user's corrections and confirmations in past Claude Code sessions, and propose them for approval. Use when the user asks to learn from their feedback, to stop repeating mistakes, "learn from my corrections" (in Italian "impara dalle mie correzioni"), or periodically after many sessions. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-instincts) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-lint**: Read-only health check of the Mnemoloci vault — stale pages whose sources changed, orphaned sources, broken wikilinks, missing frontmatter, low-confidence and contradicted pages, pending reviews, interrupted transactions. Use when the user asks about vault health or before/after large ingests. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-lint) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-maintain**: Improve the Mnemoloci wiki itself - merge duplicate pages, add missing links between pages, write synthesis pages for topics that belong together. Use when the user says "tidy up the wiki", "merge the duplicates", "link the pages", "maintain the second brain", or, in Italian, "sistema il wiki", "unisci i doppioni", "collega le pagine", "manutenzione del second brain", or when lint shows alias collisions or many orphans. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-maintain) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-new-project**: Create a brand-new project folder under the user's projects root and move the session there — typically from a Claude Code Remote Control session opened on the phone. Use when the user says "new project X", "create a project", "start a new project", or, in Italian, "nuovo progetto X", "crea un progetto", "inizia un progetto nuovo", or asks to start working on something new in its own folder. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-new-project) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-privacy**: Scan the vault for credential- or PII-shaped strings that slipped past capture-time redaction, and answer what the vault knows about a person or account. Use when the user says "check the vault for secrets", "did I write a password somewhere by mistake?", "run a privacy audit", "what do you know about me/about X", or, in Italian, "controlla se ci sono segreti nel vault", "ho scritto per sbaglio una password da qualche parte?", "fai un audit privacy", "cosa sai di me/di X", or before sharing/exporting part of the vault with someone else. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-privacy) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-query**: Answer questions from the user's Mnemoloci second brain with citations, and save good answers as wiki pages. Use when the user asks what they know, decided, or wrote about a topic ("what do I know about…", "how does… work", "what had we decided about…", in Italian "cosa so di…", "come funziona…", "cosa avevamo deciso su…"), or says "save this answer" ("salva questa risposta"). ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-query) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-remember**: Save and manage the user's personal profile, goals, and working-memory notes (decisions, lessons, handoffs, preferences, runbooks) in Mnemoloci. Use when the user says "remember that…" ("ricordati che…"), states a goal or preference, asks to track objectives, when an important decision is taken, when a mistake teaches a lesson, or before ending/pausing work to leave a handoff. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-remember) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-research**: Bounded web research on a question with the result saved in the second brain - several rounds of search, reading primary sources, looking for counter-positions, then a cited dossier; the sources become wiki pages only after the user's review. Use when the user says "research X", "dig deeper into X on the web", "what is known about X", "find sources on X", or, in Italian, "fai una ricerca su X", "approfondisci X sul web", "cosa si sa di X", "trova fonti su X". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-research) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-resume**: Find and resume past Claude Code sessions, and save a handoff for the next one. Use when the user says "let's resume", "where did we leave off", "what had we done on X", "which session did we…", "the latest sessions", or "save the session / write a handoff" before stopping, or, in Italian, "riprendiamo", "dove eravamo rimasti", "cosa avevamo fatto su X", "in quale sessione abbiamo…", "le ultime sessioni", "salva la sessione / fai un handoff". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-resume) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-review**: Show and decide Mnemoloci pages held in the review queue (low confidence, contradictions, citation problems, screenshots/imports). Use when the user wants to review pending pages or after a compile reports held pages. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-review) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-sessions**: Build initial knowledge from the user's existing Claude Code session history — decisions, preferences, project context, lessons, and facts about the user. Use when the user asks to read, learn from, or import their past Claude sessions/conversations into the second brain. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-sessions) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-similar-work**: Before starting new work, check whether something similar already exists in the vault (a past session, a compiled wiki page, or a reusable skill) so you don't reinvent it from scratch. Use when the user says "I want to implement…", "help me build…", "how do I solve…", "I want to create…", "I need to add…", "let's solve this problem" (in Italian "voglio implementare…", "aiutami a costruire…", "come risolvo…", "voglio creare…", "devo aggiungere…", "risolviamo questo problema") — not on questions about what they already know. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-similar-work) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-skills**: Find Agent Skills that already exist on this machine (Claude Code, plugins, marketplaces, ~/.agents for Kimi/Codex) and create new ones from verified knowledge, session lessons, or git history, with security scanning and a review step before install. Use when the user asks "is there already a skill for…" ("c'è già una skill per…"), wants a new skill, wants to turn a repeated workflow into a skill, or asks whether a skill is safe. ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-skills) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-today**: Todos that persist across sessions, and the daily/weekly digest of the second brain. Use when the user says "what do I have to do", "add to my to-dos", "remind me to…", "mark it done", "what did I do today / this week", "summary", "how did the week go", or, in Italian, "cosa ho da fare", "aggiungi alle cose da fare", "ricordami di…", "segna come fatto", "cosa ho fatto oggi / questa settimana", "riepilogo", "com'è andata la settimana". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-today) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-weekly**: Guided weekly review with the user (what moved, stuck goals, open questions, plan for next week) and turning documents or meeting notes into todos. Use when the user says "let's do the weekly review", "how did the week go and what do I do next", "let's plan the week", or shares a document/meeting notes and asks "what do I need to do from here", or, in Italian, "facciamo la revisione settimanale", "com'è andata la settimana e cosa faccio la prossima", "pianifichiamo la settimana", "cosa devo fare da qui". ([siris9476/mnemoloci](https://github.com/siris9476/mnemoloci/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/mnemoloci-weekly) · ★23)
- **mnemoloci-write**: Write a post, email, note or short text in the user's own voice, grounded in what their second brain knows. Use when the user says "write it the way I would", "prepare a post about X in my style", "draft an email about X", "write it in my voice", or, in Italian, "scrivi come scriverei io", "prepara un post su X con il mio stile", "bozza di email su X", "scrivilo con la mia voce".
